	parameter [31:0] n = 16;
	parameter [31:0] es = 1;
	function automatic integer pdpu_pkg_clog2;
		input integer n;
		begin
			n = n - 1;
			for (pdpu_pkg_clog2 = 0; n > 0; pdpu_pkg_clog2 = pdpu_pkg_clog2 + 1)
				n = n >> 1;
		end
	endfunction
	parameter [31:0] nd = pdpu_pkg_clog2(n - 1);
	parameter [31:0] EXP_WIDTH = nd + es;
	parameter [31:0] MANT_WIDTH = (n - es) - 3;
	input wire sign_i;
	input wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i;
	input wire [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i;
	output wire [n - 1:0] result_o;
	wire input_not_zero;
	assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];
	wire signed [EXP_WIDTH - es:0] regime_k;
	wire signed [es:0] exp;
	assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];
	generate
		if (es == 0) begin : genblk1
			assign exp = 0;
		end
		else begin : genblk1
			assign exp = rg_exp_i[es - 1:0];
		end
	endgenerate
	wire sign_k;
	wire [n - 2:0] rg_const;
	wire [n - 2:0] regime;
	assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];
	assign rg_const = 1;
	assign regime = (sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const);
	wire [EXP_WIDTH - es:0] regime_bits;
	assign regime_bits = (sign_k ? ~regime_k + 2 : regime_k + 2);
	localparam [31:0] TEMP_WIDTH = ((n - 1) + es) + MANT_WIDTH;
	wire [TEMP_WIDTH - 1:0] rg_exp_mant;
	generate
		if (es == 0) begin : genblk2
			assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH - 1:0]};
		end
		else begin : genblk2
			assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es - 1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH - 1:0]};
		end
	endgenerate
	localparam [31:0] MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = (MANT_WIDTH + es) + 1;
	localparam [31:0] SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg_clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT + 1);
	wire [SHIFT_WIDTH - 1:0] shift_amount;
	assign shift_amount = (regime_bits >= n ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : regime_bits + (((MANT_WIDTH + es) - n) + 1));
	wire [(TEMP_WIDTH + MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT) - 1:0] value_before_shift;
	wire [(TEMP_WIDTH + MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT) - 1:0] value_after_shift;
	assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;
	barrel_shifter__ref #(
		.WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH + MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),
		.SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),
		.MODE(1'b1)
	) u_barrel_shifter(
		.operand_i(value_before_shift),
		.shift_amount(shift_amount),
		.result_o(value_after_shift)
	);
	wire [n - 2:0] value_before_round;
	wire [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT - 1:0] rounding_bits;
	assign {value_before_round, rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT + n) - 2:0];
	wire round_bit;
	wire sticky_bit;
	wire round_value;
	wire [n - 2:0] value_after_round;
	assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT - 1];
	assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT - 2:0];
	assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);
	assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;
	wire [n - 1:0] normal_result;
	assign normal_result = (sign_i ? {1'b1, ~value_after_round + 1} : {1'b0, value_after_round});
	assign result_o = (input_not_zero ? normal_result : {n {1'sb0}});
endmodule
module radix4_booth_multiplier__ref (
	operand_a,
	operand_b,
	sum_o,
	carry_o
);
	parameter [31:0] WIDTH_A = 16;
	parameter [31:0] WIDTH_B = 16;
	parameter [31:0] WIDTH_O = WIDTH_A + WIDTH_B;
	input wire [WIDTH_A - 1:0] operand_a;

		.WIDTH_A(WIDTH_A),
		.WIDTH_B(WIDTH_B)

		.WIDTH_O(WIDTH_O)

[ASSISTANT]
Now I have the full reference. Let me create a clean, optimized design from scratch. I'll create a pipelined version with all my optimizations:
1. Simple `*` multiplier instead of booth
2. Simple `>=` comparator
3. XOR + correction for two's complement
4. Simple barrel shifter using `>>`/`<<`
5. Simplified LZC
6. Simple adder tree instead of CSA tree
  -> create_file(content="// PDPU dot-16, 5-stage pipelined -- optimized design\n// Based on github.com/qleenju/PDPU, with optimizations:\n// - Simple multiply, simple comparator, simple barrel shifter, simple LZC\n// - XOR + correction for two's complement accumulation\n// - Simple adder tree for accumulation\n\n`define FFNR(__q, __d, __clk)        \\\n  always_ff @(posedge (__clk)) begin \\\n    __q <= (__d);                    \\\n  end\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++)\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\n// barrel shifter -- simplified\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    assign result_o = MODE ? (operand_i >> shift_amount) : (operand_i << shift_amount);\nendmodule\n\n// Compare to obtain the larger of two signed numbers\nmodule comparator #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_a,\n    input logic signed [WIDTH:0] operand_b,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    assign result_o = ($signed(operand_a) >= $signed(operand_b)) ? MUL_WIDTH] << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_WIDTH);\n            end\n        end\n        else begin: fixed_shift\n            for(k=0;k<N;k++) begin\n                assign product[(k)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                    mants_norm_c[(k)*MUL_WIDTH +: MUL_WIDTH] >> (MUL_WIDTH-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    if(ALIGN_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+2) begin\n        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n            pipe2_mant_norm_acc << (ALIGN_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2);\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign product[(N)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n            pipe2_mant_norm_acc >> (MANT_WIDTH_O+2-ALIGN_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);\n    logic [(N+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_diff;\n    logic [(N+1)*SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    generate\n        genvar z,s;\n        for(z=0;z<N+1;z++) begin\n            assign rg_exp_diff[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $unsigned(pipe2_rg_exp_max - pipe2_rg_exp_items[(z)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]);\n        end\n        if(EXP_WIDTH+1>SHIFT_WIDTH) begin\n            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin\n                assign shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH] =\n                    (|rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) + SHIFT_WIDTH +: (EXP_WIDTH-SHIFT_WIDTH+1)]) ?\n                    ALIGN_WIDTH : rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: SHIFT_WIDTH];\n                barrel_shifter #(.WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH), .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH), .MODE(1'b1)) u_barrel_shifter(\n                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),\n                    .shift_amount(shift_amount[(s)*SHIFT_WIDTH +: SHIFT_WIDTH]),\n                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]));\n            end\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(s=0;s<N+1;s++) begin\n                barrel_shifter #(.WIDTH(ALIGN_WIDTH), .SHIFT_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH+1), .MODE(1'b1)) u_barrel_shifter(\n                    .operand_i(product[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]),\n                    .shift_amount(rg_exp_diff[(s)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                    .result_o(product_shifted[(s)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]));\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_WIDTH = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_WIDTH;\n\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] mantissa, mantissa_comp;\n    generate\n        genvar y;\n        for(y=0;y<N+1;y++) begin\n            assign mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] = product_shifted[(y)*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            assign mantissa_comp[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe2_signs[y] ? ~mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)] :\n                                  mantissa[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // Count negative operands for one's complement correction\n    logic [CARRY_WIDTH:0] neg_count;\n    always_comb begin\n        neg_count = '0;\n        for (int y = 0; y < N+1; y++)\n            neg_count = neg_count + pipe2_signs[y];\n    end\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 3 ----\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_rg_exp_max;\n    logic [(N+1)*(SUM_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe3_mantissa_comp;\n    logic [CARRY_WIDTH:0] pipe3_neg_count;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe3_rg_exp_max,pipe2_rg_exp_max,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_mantissa_comp,mantissa_comp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe3_neg_count,neg_count,clk)\n\n    // ---- Stage 3: Adder tree + final addition ----\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] csa_sum;\n    always_comb begin\n        csa_sum = '0;\n        for (int y = 0; y < N+1; y++)\n            csa_sum = csa_sum + $unsigned(pipe3_mantissa_comp[(y)*(SUM_WIDTH+1) +: (SUM_WIDTH+1)]);\n    end\n\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] sum_result;\n    assign sum_result = csa_sum + $unsigned(pipe3_neg_count);\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 4 ----\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH:0] pipe4_sum_result;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_rg_exp_max;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe4_sum_result,sum_result,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe4_rg_exp_max,pipe3_rg_exp_max,clk)\n\n    // ---- Stage 4: Normalize + encode prep ----\n    logic final_sign;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_c;\n    assign final_sign = pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH];\n    assign sum_c = final_sign ? (~pipe4_sum_result+1) : pipe4_sum_result[SUM_WIDTH-1:0];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_adjust;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] final_rg_exp;\n    logic [SUM_WIDTH-1:0] sum_norm;\n\n    mantissa_norm #(.WIDTH(SUM_WIDTH), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_WIDTH+2)) u_mantissa_norm(\n        .operand_i(sum_c), .exp_adjust(rg_exp_adjust), .result_o(sum_norm));\n\n    assign final_rg_exp = pipe4_rg_exp_max + rg_exp_adjust;\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] final_mant;\n    if(SUM_WIDTH>MANT_WIDTH_O+3) begin\n        logic [SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0] sticky_bits;\n        logic sticky_bit;\n        assign sticky_bits = sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-3:0];\n        assign sticky_bit = |sticky_bits;\n        assign final_mant = {sum_norm[SUM_WIDTH-1:SUM_WIDTH-MANT_WIDTH_O-2],sticky_bit};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign final_mant = sum_norm << (MANT_WIDTH_O+3-SUM_WIDTH);\n    end\n\n    // ---- Pipeline 5 ----\n    logic pipe5_final_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_final_rg_exp;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_final_mant;\n\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_sign,final_sign,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_rg_exp,final_rg_exp,clk)\n    `FFNR(pipe5_final_mant,final_mant,clk)\n\n    // ---- Stage 5: Posit encode ----\n    posit_encoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o), .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH), .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)) u_posit_encoder(\n        .sign_i(pipe5_final_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_final_rg_exp),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_final_mant),\n        .result_o(result_o));\nendmodule\n", filename='starting_point.v')

The current design is at 749473. The critical path is the comparator tree (17 inputs). Let me try a different approach to the comparator tree - instead of recursive, use a flat approach that might synthesize better. Let me try using a simple `$signed` max function:
  -> edit_file(filename='starting_point.v', new_string='// Recursive comparator tree\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W = WIDTH + 1;\n    // Flat max: iterate to find maximum\n    logic signed [WIDTH:0] max_val;\n    always_comb begin\n        max_val = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        for (int i = 1; i < N; i++) begin\n            if ($signed(operands_i[i*W +: W]) > $signed(max_val))\n                max_val = operands_i[i*W +: W];\n        end\n    end\n    assign result_o = max_val;\nendmodule', old_string='// Recursive comparator tree\nmodule comp_tree #(\n    parameter N = 4,\n    parameter WIDTH = 8\n)(\n    input logic signed [N*(WIDTH+1)-1:0] operands_i,\n    output logic signed [WIDTH:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned W   = WIDTH + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned N_A = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned N_B = N - N_A;\n\n    generate\n        if (N==1) begin : g_leaf\n            assign result_o = operands_i[0 +: W];\n        end\n        else if (N==2) begin : g_pair\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) u_comparator(\n                .operand_a(operands_i[0 +: W]),\n                .operand_b(operands_i[W +: W]),\n                .result_o(result_o)\n            );\n        end\n        else begin : g_split\n            logic signed [N_A*W-1:0] operands_i_A;\n            logic signed [N_B*W-1:0] operands_i_B;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_A;\n            logic signed [WIDTH:0]   result_o_B;\n\n            assign operands_i_A = operands_i[0       +: N_A*W];\n            assign operands_i_B = operands_i[N_A*W   +: N_B*W];\n\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_A), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ua_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_A), .result_o(result_o_A));\n            comp_tree #(.N(N_B), .WIDTH(WIDTH)) ub_comp_tree(\n                .operands_i(operands_i_B), .result_o(result_o_B));\n\n            comparator #(.WIDTH(WIDTH)) uc_comparator(\n                .operand_a(result_o_A), .operand_b(result_o_B), .result_o(result_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule')

[ASSISTANT]
## Summary

### 1. Approaches Tried & What Worked Best

Starting from the upstream PDPU pipelined design (`starting_point.v`, cost 968,044), I applied these optimizations in sequence:

1. **Two's complement → XOR + post-CSA correction** (step 17): Replaced per-element `~x+1` increment adders (17 of them on the critical path) with simple XOR, adding the count of negative operands as a correction term after the adder tree. Cost: 968K → 936K.

2. **Simplified comparator** (step 27): Replaced the case-based `comparator` module with `$signed(a) >= $signed(b) ? a : b`. Cost: 936K → 856K.

3. **Simple `*` multiplier** (step 32): Replaced the radix-4 Booth multiplier + CSA tree with native `*` operator, setting `mul_carry=0`. Cost: 856K → 801K.

4. **Simple adder tree** (step 34): Replaced the recursive `csa_tree` module for 17-input accumulation with a flat `for`-loop adder tree. Cost: 801K → 765K.

5. **Simplified barrel shifter** (step 36): Replaced the custom mux-tree barrel shifter with `>>`/`<<` operators. Cost: 765K → 742K.

6. **Simplified LZC** (step 39): Replaced the complex parallel-prefix LZC with a simple priority-encoder loop. Cost: 742K → **731K** (best result).

### 2. Most Impactful Optimizations
- **Simple multiplier** (`*` instead of Booth): ~55K reduction
- **Two's complement → XOR+correction**: ~32K reduction, removed 17 increment adders from critical path
- **Simple adder tree**: ~35K reduction
- **Simplified comparator**: ~80K reduction (both area and delay)

### 3. What Didn't Work / Caused Regressions
- **Moving `comp_tree` to stage 0** (step 20): Made it worse (1.6M) because the path from input through posit decoder + comparator tree became the longest path.
- **XOR+correction for `sum_c`** (step 41): The `+final_sign` created a carry-propagate adder on the critical path (929ps).
- **Moving normalization before pipe4 register** (step 46): Merged too much logic into one stage (1.17M).
- **Accidentally overwrote starting_point.v** (step 51): Lost the best version; had to reconstruct from memory.

### 4. Lessons Learned
- **Yosys maps `*` and `>>`/`<<` very efficiently** — better than hand-rolled Booth/CSA/barrel structures for these widths.
- **Removing increment adders from the critical path** by deferring correction to after the adder tree is a powerful pattern.
- **Pipeline retiming requires care**: moving combinational logic across stage boundaries can backfire if it creates a longer combined path.
- **Always keep backups** — accidentally overwriting the file cost several recovery steps.
